```json
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 幂等键 `m10:idem:rx:submit:{key}`；2) 校验 `m10:rx:update`；3) 状态必须 `DRAFT`/`REJECTED`，`version` 乐观锁；4) 必填完整性校验（双眼明细、矫正视力、瞳距、渐进片 ADD/瞳高）；5) 执行预检，存在 ERROR 命中 → `"1002038"`；6) 若 `supply_check_status='NOT_CHECKED'` 且配置要求校验 → `"1002021"`；7) **生成 `rx_no`**（Redis `INCR m10:code:RX:{yyyyMMdd}`，DB 唯一索引兜底，失败 `"1002017"`）；8) 判定 `audit_level`（儿童首次/渐进/棱镜 → `DOUBLE`/`SENIOR`；否则按配置可能 `SELF`）；9) 创建 `m10_prescription_audit`（`audit_result='PENDING'`，含 `rule_hits` 快照与 `rule_version`）；10) 置 `status='PENDING_AUDIT'`，`audit_round = 上一轮 + 1`；11) 写日志 `SUBMIT_AUDIT`；12) 若 `audit_level='DOUBLE'` 推送消息通知第二位审核人（U-12）。

#### API-12 审核（通过 / 带说明通过 / 驳回）

**请求**

```
POST /api/v1/m10/prescriptions/RX202610310011/audit
Idempotency-Key: 7c6b5a4d-3e2f-4a1b-9c8d-7e6f5a4b3c2d
```

```json
{
  "version": 3,
  "audit_result": "PASS_WITH_NOTE",
  "audit_note": "渐进片 ADD +2.00 与瞳高已核对，轴位接近垂直方向已建议复查，本次按验光单数据通过。",
  "reject_reason": null,
  "reject_codes": null,
  "overrides": [
    { "rule_code": "RAR012", "remark": "轴位 90° 已用交叉柱镜复核，确认无误，予以通过。" }
  ]
}
```

**响应（成功 — 带说明通过）**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "rx_no": "RX202610310011",
    "status": "AUDITED",
    "status_label": "已审核",
    "audit_no": "RXA2026103100013",
    "audit_result": "PASS_WITH_NOTE",
    "audit_round": 1,
    "audited_at": "2026-10-31T09:35:02+08:00",
    "audit_duration_sec": 1938,
    "valid_from": "2026-10-31",
    "valid_until": "2027-10-31",
    "sign_hash": "b8e4d2f1a3c5e7b9d0f2a4c6e8b0d1f39a7f3c1e9b60d4a8f2c5e7b1d3f9a0c2e",
    "need_second_audit": false,
    "event_published": "m10.prescription.issued"
  },
  "trace_id": "a1b2c3d4e5f6a7b8"
}
```

**响应（成功 — 儿童首次配镜，需第二人复核）**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "rx_no": "RX202610310013",
    "status": "PENDING_AUDIT",
    "status_label": "待双人复核",
    "audit_no": "RXA2026103100014",
    "audit_result": "PASS",
    "audit_round": 1,
    "need_second_audit": true,
    "second_audit_note": "儿童首次配镜需另一名审核人复核后才签发",
    "audited_at": "2026-10-31T09:40:11+08:00"
  },
  "trace_id": "a1b2c3d4e5f6a7b8"
}
```

**响应（失败 — 存在 ERROR 命中）**

```json
{
  "code": "1002011",
  "message": "存在错误级规则命中，不可审核通过：RAR003 儿童首次配镜未散瞳",
  "data": {
    "error_hits": [
      { "rule_code": "RAR003", "rule_name": "儿童首次配镜必须散瞳", "severity": "ERROR",
        "eye_side": "BOTH", "field": "pupil_dilated", "actual": "false", "expected": "true",
        "error_code": "1002005",
        "message": "12 岁以下首次配镜必须先散瞳验光",
        "suggestion": "请安排散瞳验光后重新开立，或由科主任申请越权放行" }
    ],
    "override_allowed": false
  },
  "trace_id": "a1b2c3d4e5f6a7b8"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 幂等键 `m10:idem:rx:audit:{key}`；2) 校验 `m10:rx:audit`；3) 状态必须 `PENDING_AUDIT`，否则 `"1002006"`；`version` 乐观锁；4) 取本轮 `PENDING` 审核记录，重新执行规则（用当前规则集，命中结果覆盖旧快照）；5) **审核人不得为自己开立的处方**（`audit_level='SELF'` 除外），否则 `"1002011"`；6) `audit_result='REJECT'` 时 `reject_reason` ≥ 10 字，否则 `"1002013"`；7) `audit_result` 为通过类时：存在 ERROR → `"1002011"`（除非已走 API-13 越权）；存在 WARN 且无 `audit_note` → `"1002010"`；8) 双人复核：若 `audit_level='DOUBLE'` 且本轮为第一位审核人，则仅记录审核并保持 `PENDING_AUDIT`，`need_second_audit=true`；第二位审核人通过后才置 `AUDITED`；9) 置 `AUDITED` 时写 `issued_by` / `issued_at` / `audited_by` / `audited_result` / `sign_hash`（SHA-256 of `to_sign_payload()`）；10) `REJECT` 时置 `REJECTED`，`audit_round` 保留，`reject_reason` 冗余到主表；11) 写日志 `AUDIT_PASS` / `AUDIT_REJECT`；12) 置 `AUDITED` 后发布事件 `m10.prescription.issued`，并置 `PASS`/`PASS_WITH_NOTE` 事件 `m10.prescription.audited`（供 M10-06）；13) 计算 `audit_duration_sec`。

#### API-13 越权放行申请

**请求**

```json
{
  "version": 3,
  "override_reason": "患者因角膜屈光手术史需散瞳禁忌，已由李主任组织会诊确认等效屈光数据可替代，特申请放行。",
  "override_approval_no": "SP202610310007"
}
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "rx_no": "RX202610310013",
    "status": "AUDITED",
    "status_label": "已审核",
    "is_override": true,
    "override_approved_by_name": "李主任",
    "override_approval_no": "SP202610310007",
    "audited_at": "2026-10-31T10:12:44+08:00",
    "need_second_audit": false,
    "qc_flag": "OVERRIDE_PASS",
    "event_published": "m10.prescription.issued"
  },
  "trace_id": "a1b2c3d4e5f6a7b8"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 校验 `m10:rx:approve`；2) 存在 ERROR 命中且规则 `is_override_allowed=TRUE`；3) `override_reason` ≥ 20 字（`"1002012"`）；4) `override_approval_no` 必填并调 M20 校验审批单有效（`"1002012"`）；5) 写入 `m10_prescription_audit.is_override=TRUE` 及审批人/单号；6) 置 `AUDITED` 并签发；7) 写日志 `OVERRIDE_PASS`；8) 标记质控 `qc_flag`，进入 P08 质控「越权放行」清单；9) 发布 `m10.prescription.issued` 与 `m10.prescription.audited` 事件。

#### API-14 撤回审核

**请求**

```json
{ "version": 3, "withdraw_reason": "发现验光单号选错，撤回后重新选择" }
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "rx_no": "RX202610310013",
    "status": "DRAFT",
    "status_label": "草稿",
    "withdrawn_audit_no": "RXA2026103100015",
    "version": 4
  },
  "trace_id": "a1b2c3d4e5f6a7b8"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 校验 `m10:rx:update`；2) 状态必须 `PENDING_AUDIT`；3) `audit_round` 必须为 0（首轮且尚未有审核结论），否则 `"1002006"`；4) 逻辑删除本轮 `PENDING` 审核记录（`is_deleted=TRUE`，保留轨迹）；5) 置 `DRAFT`，`rx_no` **保留不释放**（避免号段回退导致下游引用失效）；6) 写日志。

#### API-15 作废处方

**请求**

```json
{ "version": 4, "void_reason": "患者决定暂不配镜，要求作废本次处方。" }
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "rx_no": "RX202610310013",
    "status": "VOID",
    "status_label": "已作废",
    "voided_at": "2026-10-31T11:02:10+08:00",
    "referenced_orders": []
  },
  "trace_id": "a1b2c3d4e5f6a7b8"
}
```

**响应（失败 — 已被订单引用）**

```json
{
  "code": "1002027",
  "message": "该处方已被配镜订单引用（PO202610310021），不可作废",
  "data": { "orders": [ { "order_no": "PO202610310021", "order_status": "PAID" } ] },
  "trace_id": "a1b2c3d4e5f6a7b8"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 校验 `m10:rx:update`（`AUDITED` 状态需 `m10:rx:approve`）；2) `void_reason` ≥ 10 字（`"1002013"`）；3) 查 `m10_order` 是否存在引用（`order.rx_no = 本处方号` 且非 `CANCELLED`），有则 `"1002027"`；4) 置 `VOID`，写 `void_reason`/`voided_by`/`voided_at`；5) 若原为 `AUDITED`，`superseded_by_rx_id` 必须已有值或该处方从未被使用（数据库约束 `ck_m10_rx_void_issued` 兜底）；6) 写日志 `VOID`。

#### API-16 复验换发

**请求**

```
POST /api/v1/m10/prescriptions/RX202610310012/renew
Idempotency-Key: 2d3e4f5a-6b7c-4d8e-9f0a-1b2c3d4e5f6a
```

```json
{
  "old_rx_no": "RX202610310012",
  "new_optometry_no": "YG202704150008",
  "validity_months": 6,
  "renew_reason": "半年复验，近视进展 0.50D，按新验光数据换发新处方。"
}
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "new_rx_no": "RX202704150021",
    "old_rx_no": "RX202610310012",
    "chain_root_rx_id": 88135,
    "sequence_no": 2,
    "status": "PENDING_AUDIT",
    "status_label": "待审核",
    "valid_from": "2027-04-15",
    "valid_until": "2027-10-15",
    "deviation_max_sph": 0.25,
    "sphere_equivalent_delta": { "OD": -0.50, "OS": -0.50 },
    "progression_alert": true,
    "next_step": "SUPPLY_CHECK_THEN_AUDIT"
  },
  "trace_id": "a1b2c3d4e5f6a7b8"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 幂等键；2) 校验 `m10:rx:create`；3) 原处方必须为 `AUDITED` 或 `EXPIRED`，否则 `"1002018"`；4) `renew_reason` ≥ 10 字（`"1002018"`）；5) 若提供新验光单，调 U-01 取新验光值并自动生成新处方度数（医师可改），计算偏差与新 SE；6) 新处方复制原处方的镜片要素、瞳距结构，`sequence_no = 原 sequence_no + 1`，`chain_root_rx_id = 原 chain_root_rx_id`（若原无根则取原 ID），`renewed_from_rx_id = 原 ID`；7) 生成新 `rx_no` 并创建 `PENDING` 审核记录；8) **原处方 `superseded_by_rx_id` 指向新处方**（新处方审核通过后才生效，审核驳回则回滚该字段）；9) SE 变化 > 0.50D 时 `progression_alert=true`，供 M10-09 建档参考；10) 写日志 `RENEW`。

#### API-17 补开处方

**请求**

```json
{
  "original_rx_no": "RX202610310012",
  "reissue_reason": "处方单遗失，患者申请重新出具一份纸质处方笺。",
  "print_immediately": true
}
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "new_rx_no": "RX202610310020",
    "original_rx_no": "RX202610310012",
    "reissue_of_rx_id": 88135,
    "chain_root_rx_id": 88135,
    "sequence_no": 2,
    "status": "AUDITED",
    "status_label": "已审核",
    "valid_until": "2027-04-30",
    "watermark": null,
    "print_job": { "print_no": "PRT202610310044", "copies": 1 }
  },
  "trace_id": "a1b2c3d4e5f6a7b8"
}
```

**响应（原处方已过期时的补开）**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "new_rx_no": "RX202610310021",
    "original_rx_no": "RX202510310003",
    "status": "AUDITED",
    "watermark": "已过期，仅供核对",
    "print_job": { "print_no": "PRT202610310045", "copies": 1 }
  },
  "trace_id": "a1b2c3d4e5f6a7b8"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 校验 `m10:rx:create`；2) 原处方必须 `AUDITED` 或 `EXPIRED`（`"1002026"`）；3) `reissue_reason` ≥ 10 字（`"1002019"`）；4) 查订单引用，存在则 `"1002027"`；5) 复制原处方内容（**度数一字不改**），`reissue_of_rx_id = 原 ID`，`sequence_no = 原 + 1`；6) 因内容与原完全一致，直接复用原审核结论与签名（新记录独立 `sign_hash` 但内容摘要相同），置 `AUDITED`；7) 原处方为 `EXPIRED` 时，补开件 `watermark='已过期，仅供核对'`，且 **P-05 返回 `is_valid=false`**，不允许据此下单；8) `print_immediately=true` 时调 API-18 打印；9) 写日志 `REISSUE`。

#### API-18 签署并打印

**请求**

```json
{
  "sign_type": "CA_CERT",
  "sign_cert_sn": "CERT-2026-000412",
  "copies": 1,
  "layout": "A5_LANDSCAPE",
  "with_qrcode": true,
  "send_to_patient": false
}
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "rx_no": "RX202610310012",
    "print_no": "PRT202610310044",
    "print_count": 1,
    "last_printed_at": "2026-10-31T09:45:20+08:00",
    "sign_hash": "a7f3c1e9b60d4a8f2c5e7b1d3f9a0c2e4b6d8f1a3c5e7b9d0f2a4c6e8b0d1f39",
    "pdf_url": "/api/v1/m10/prescriptions/RX202610310012/pdf?print_no=PRT202610310044",
    "qrcode_content": "RX202610310012|a7f3c1e9"
  },
  "trace_id": "a1b2c3d4e5f6a7b8"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 校验 `m10:rx:print`；2) 状态必须 `AUDITED` 或 `EXPIRED`，否则 `"1002011"`；3) `copies` ≤ `M10.RX.PRINT_MAX_COPIES`（默认 3），否则 `"1002035"`；4) `sign_type='CA_CERT'` 时校验证书有效（过期/缺失 `"1002036"`）；5) 重新计算内容摘要并与落库 `sign_hash` 比对，不一致 `"1002037"`；6) 渲染 PDF（A5 横向，含医院抬头、处方号、患者、按眼别度数表、镜片要素、瞳距、有效期、临床说明、开方医师与审核人签名位、签名摘要、二维码）；**PDF 不含任何金额**；7) `print_count + 1`，写 `last_printed_at`；8) `EXPIRED` 状态打印件加「已过期」水印；9) 写日志 `PRINT`；10) `send_to_patient=true` 时调 M03-05 推送 PDF。

```
GET /api/v1/m10/prescriptions/templates?owner_scope=PERSONAL&template_kind=DOSAGE
    &lens_type=SINGLE_VISION&page=1&page_size=20
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "items": [
      { "id": 312, "template_code": "RXT00012",
        "template_name": "中度近视+轻度散光 单光 1.60",
        "template_kind": "DOSAGE", "owner_scope": "PERSONAL",
        "lens_type": "SINGLE_VISION", "lens_type_label": "单光",
        "refractive_index_req": 1.60, "blue_light_block_req": true,
        "pd_total_default": 62.00, "usage_count": 186,
        "last_used_at": "2026-10-30T15:12:33+08:00", "status": "ENABLED",
        "items": [
          { "eye_side": "OD", "sphere": -3.00, "cylinder": -0.50, "axis": 180,
            "va_cc": 1.00, "refractive_index": 1.60 },
          { "eye_side": "OS", "sphere": -3.25, "cylinder": -0.75, "axis": 175,

        ] }
    ],
    "total": 12,
    "page": 1,
    "page_size": 20,
    "pages": 1
  },
  "trace_id": "a1b2c3d4e5f6a7b8"

```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 校验 `m10:rx:view`；2) 可见性过滤：`owner_scope='PERSONAL' AND owner_id=当前人` OR `owner_scope='DEPT' AND owner_dept_id IN (我的科室)` OR `owner_scope='HOSPITAL'`；3) 默认按 `usage_count DESC, last_used_at DESC`（「常用模板」排序）；4) 走 `idx_m10_rx_tpl_owner` / `idx_m10_rx_tpl_usage`；5) 返回 `items_json` 展开后的明细。

#### API-20 新建模板

**请求**

```json
{
  "template_name": "儿童离焦镜片标准处方",
  "template_kind": "DOSAGE",
  "owner_scope": "DEPT",
  "owner_dept_id": 302,

  "refractive_index_req": 1.60,
  "anti_uv_req": true,
  "special_req": "离焦区不可削边，镜架框宽 ≥ 46mm",
  "items": [
    { "eye_side": "OD", "sphere": -3.50, "cylinder": 0, "axis": null,
      "va_cc": 1.00, "refractive_index": 1.60 },
    { "eye_side": "OS", "sphere": -3.25, "cylinder": 0, "axis": null,
      "va_cc": 1.00, "refractive_index": 1.60 }
  ],
  "pd_total_default": 58.00
}
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "id": 313,
    "template_code": "RXT00013",
    "owner_scope": "DEPT",
    "owner_dept_id": 302,
    "version": 0,
    "status": "ENABLED"
  },
  "trace_id": "a1b2c3d4e5f6a7b8"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 幂等键；2) 校验 `m10:rx:create`；3) `template_kind='DOSAGE'` 必须有明细，否则 `"1002029"`；`owner_scope='PERSONAL'` 必须有归属人（自动填当前人），`DEPT` 必须有归属科室，否则 `"1002029"`；4) 生成 `template_code`（`RXT` + 5 位，Redis `m10:code:RXT`）；5) 明细存入 `items_json`；6) 写日志。

#### API-21 修改模板

**请求**

```json
{ "version": 0, "template_name": "儿童离焦镜片标准处方 V2", "refractive_index_req": 1.67,
  "items": [ { "eye_side": "OD", "sphere": -3.50, "cylinder": 0, "va_cc": 1.00 },
             { "eye_side": "OS", "sphere": -3.25, "cylinder": 0, "va_cc": 1.00 } ] }
```

**响应**

```json
{ "code": 0, "message": "success",
  "data": { "template_code": "RXT00013", "version": 1 }, "trace_id": "a1b2c3d4e5f6a7b8" }
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 校验 `m10:rx:update`；2) 模板必须存在且可见，否则 `"1002028"`；3) 个人模板仅归属人可改，科室模板需本科室 `m10:rx:update`，否则 `"109001"`；4) `version` 乐观锁，冲突 `"40900"`；5) 校验明细与归属，否则 `"1002029"`。

#### API-22 启停模板

**请求**

```
PATCH /api/v1/m10/prescriptions/templates/313/status
```

```json
{ "status": "DISABLED", "version": 1, "reason": "老旧处方结构，已由新模板替代" }
```

**响应**

```json
{ "code": 0, "message": "success",
  "data": { "template_code": "RXT00013", "status": "DISABLED", "version": 2 },
  "trace_id": "a1b2c3d4e5f6a7b8" }
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 校验 `m10:rx:update`；2) 可见性与归属校验（`"1002028"` / `"109001"`）；3) 置 `DISABLED`（停用不删除，历史引用仍可读）；4) `version` 乐观锁；5) 写日志。

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "rx_id": 88135,
    "rx_no": "RX202610310012",
    "check_no": "RSC202610310000022",
    "supply_check_status": "CHECK_FAILED",
    "is_degraded": true,
    "checked_at": "2026-10-31T10:05:40+08:00",
    "eyes": [
      { "eye_side": "OD", "is_available": null, "is_degraded": true,
        "message": "光度校验服务暂不可用，请人工确认度数能否供应" },
      { "eye_side": "OS", "is_available": null, "is_degraded": true,
        "message": "光度校验服务暂不可用，请人工确认度数能否供应" }
    ],
    "alternatives": [],
    "is_blocking": false,
    "message": "M01-08 视光产品服务暂不可用，已记录降级，不阻断开方与审核"
  },
  "trace_id": "a1b2c3d4e5f6a7b8"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 校验 `m10:rx:view`；2) 取处方主表与明细；3) 确定校验商品：若请求给了 `product_code` 用一个商品，否则按镜片类型取「默认校验商品」（`m10_prescription_config` 中配置的镜片类型→代表商品映射，缺省用 M01-08 `eligible` 取第一个）；4) 调 U-04 `POST /internal/v1/optical/specs/lookup`（逐眼，或 U-05 批量）；5) 解析返回：`found` / `is_available` / `supply_type` / `lead_time_days` / `final_price`（**直接取 M01-08 值，本模块不计算任何价格**）；6) 不可供时判定 `unavailable_reason`（`OUT_OF_RANGE` / `PRODUCT_DISABLED` / `TRIAL_LENS` / `SPEC_NOT_CONFIGURED`）并生成 `suggestion_kind`（`UPGRADE_INDEX` / `CHANGE_DESIGN` / `SPLIT_ORDER` / `REDUCE_CYL`）；7) 调 U-07 `products/eligible` 取 2~5 个替换候选编码存入 `alternative_product_codes`（**这是 M10-05 替换推荐的上游依据**）；8) 按 `M10.RX.SUPPLY_BLOCK_ON_UNAVAILABLE` 决定 `is_blocking`；9) M01-08 不可用时 `is_degraded=TRUE`、`is_available=NULL`、`is_blocking=FALSE`，提示人工确认；10) 每次都落 `m10_prescription_supply_check` 快照（含 `optical_version`、`cost_ms`、`trace_id`）；11) 回写主表 `supply_check_status` / `supply_check_at` / `unavailable_eyes` 与明细 `supply_check_status` / `supply_type` / `lead_time_days`；12) 写日志 `SUPPLY_CHECK`。

#### API-24 取最近一次可供性校验结果

**请求**

```
GET /api/v1/m10/prescriptions/RX202610310012/supply-check/latest
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "rx_no": "RX202610310012",
    "has_result": true,
    "check_no": "RSC202610310000018",
    "supply_check_status": "ALL_AVAILABLE",
    "supply_check_version": "v42:20261030-1830",
    "checked_at": "2026-10-31T09:16:40+08:00",
    "is_stale": false,
    "stale_hours": 0,
    "optical_version_changed": false,
    "eyes": [
      { "eye_side": "OD", "is_available": true, "supply_type": "STOCK", "lead_time_days": null },
      { "eye_side": "OS", "is_available": true, "supply_type": "CUSTOM", "lead_time_days": 7 }
    ]
  },
  "trace_id": "a1b2c3d4e5f6a7b8"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 校验 `m10:rx:view`；2) 走部分索引 `idx_m10_rx_sc_latest`（`is_degraded=FALSE`）取每眼最近一条；3) `is_stale` = 距上次校验超过 `M10.RX.SUPPLY_RECHECK_HOURS`（72h）；4) 调 U-06 `optical/version` 与快照中的 `optical_version` 比对，不相等则 `optical_version_changed=true`（提示 M01-08 主数据已变更，建议重新校验）；5) 无任何校验记录时 `has_result=false`。

#### API-25 审核规则列表

**请求**

```
GET /api/v1/m10/prescriptions/audit-rules?status=ENABLED&rule_category=DEVIATION
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "items": [
      { "rule_code": "RAR003", "rule_name": "儿童首次配镜必须散瞳", "rule_category": "CHILD",
        "severity": "ERROR", "error_code": "1002005",
        "expression": "age <= AGE_CHILD_MAX AND is_first_fitting = TRUE AND pupil_dilated = FALSE",
        "params": { "AGE_CHILD_MAX": 12 }, "eye_scope": "BOTH",
        "age_scope_min": 0, "age_scope_max": 12,
        "is_override_allowed": true, "override_role_codes": "MEDICAL_DIRECTOR,CHIEF_PHYSICIAN",
        "suggestion_text": "请安排散瞳验光后重新开立处方",
        "priority": 10, "version_tag": "V1.0", "status": "ENABLED" },
      { "rule_code": "RAR005", "rule_name": "处方与验光球镜偏差", "rule_category": "DEVIATION",
        "severity": "WARN", "error_code": "1002007",
        "expression": "abs(deviation_sph) > DEV_SPH_MAX",
        "params": { "DEV_SPH_MAX": 0.5 }, "eye_scope": "EACH",
        "is_override_allowed": false,
        "suggestion_text": "请在偏差说明中写明临床调整原因",
        "priority": 20, "version_tag": "V1.0", "status": "ENABLED" }
    ],
    "total": 32,
    "rule_version": "V1.0"
  },
  "trace_id": "a1b2c3d4e5f6a7b8"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 校验 `m10:rx:config`；2) 按 `org_id` 取本院区规则（无本院区覆盖则取 `org_id=1` 默认）；3) 支持 `status` / `rule_category` 筛选；4) 按 `priority` 升序返回；5) 同时返回汇总的 `rule_version`（所有启用规则的 `version_tag` 集合摘要）。

#### API-26 修改审核规则

**请求**

```
PUT /api/v1/m10/prescriptions/audit-rules/RAR005
```

```json
{
  "rule_name": "处方与验光球镜偏差",
  "rule_category": "DEVIATION",
  "severity": "WARN",
  "error_code": "1002007",
  "expression": "abs(deviation_sph) > DEV_SPH_MAX",
  "params": { "DEV_SPH_MAX": 0.75 },
  "eye_scope": "EACH",
  "suggestion_text": "请在偏差说明中写明临床调整原因",
  "priority": 20,
  "status": "ENABLED",
  "change_reason": "依据医务科讨论，将球镜偏差阈值由 0.50D 放宽至 0.75D"
}
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "rule_code": "RAR005",
    "version_tag": "V1.1",
    "changed_fields": ["params", "version_tag"],
    "need_medical_approval": false,
    "cache_invalidated": true
  },
  "trace_id": "a1b2c3d4e5f6a7b8"
}
```

**响应（失败 — 错误码越界）**

```json
{
  "code": "1002034",
  "message": "审核规则编码或错误码越界：error_code 1002099 不在本子模块号段 1002001~1002040",
  "data": { "allowed_error_code_range": ["1002001", "1002040"] },
  "trace_id": "a1b2c3d4e5f6a7b8"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 校验 `m10:rx:config`；2) `severity='ERROR'` 且无 `error_code` → `"1002033"`；3) `error_code` 必须落在 `1002001`~`1002040`（`"1002034"`）；`rule_code` 必须匹配 `RAR001`~`RAR060`（`"1002034"`）；4) `is_override_allowed=TRUE` 必须有 `override_role_codes`（`"1002033"`）；5) 修改 ERROR 级规则需 `m10:rx:approve`（否则 `"109001"`）；6) `version_tag` 次版本号 +1（如 `V1.0` → `V1.1`）；7) 失效 Redis 规则缓存 `m10:rx:rules:{org_id}*`；8) 写日志并调 M02-06 审计；9) 返回 `cache_invalidated=true`。

#### API-28 修改系统参数

**请求**

```json
{
  "items": [
    { "config_key": "M10.RX.VALIDITY_MONTHS_CHILD", "config_value": "6",
      "reason": "维持 6 个月，与近视防控随访频次一致" },
    { "config_key": "M10.RX.DEVIATION_SPH_MAX", "config_value": "0.75",
      "reason": "放宽球镜偏差阈值至 0.75D" }
  ],
  "approval_no": "SP202610310012"
}
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "updated": ["M10.RX.DEVIATION_SPH_MAX"],
    "unchanged": ["M10.RX.VALIDITY_MONTHS_CHILD"],
    "cache_invalidated": true,
    "need_approval": false
  },
  "trace_id": "a1b2c3d4e5f6a7b8"
}
```

**响应（失败 — 有效期参数越界）**

```json
{
  "code": "1002022",
  "message": "处方有效期参数不合法：儿童有效期必须为 1~36 个月（当前值 0）",
  "data": { "config_key": "M10.RX.VALIDITY_MONTHS_CHILD", "min_value": 1, "max_value": 36 },
  "trace_id": "a1b2c3d4e5f6a7b8"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 校验 `m10:rx:config`；2) 逐项校验：`is_editable=FALSE` 拒绝（`"109001"`）；类型合法性（`INT` 必须整数、`BOOLEAN` 必须 `TRUE`/`FALSE`）；数值区间（`min_value`~`max_value`）；有效期类参数强制 1~36（`"1002022"`）；3) `is_sensitive=TRUE` 的参数修改必须携带有效 `approval_no`，否则 `"1002040"`；4) 值有变化的才写入，`version+1`；5) 失效 Redis `m10:rx:config:{org_id}` 缓存；6) 写日志并调 M02-06 审计。

```json
{

             "status": ["AUDITED"], "lens_type": ["SINGLE_VISION", "PROGRESSIVE"] },

              "sphere_od", "cylinder_od", "axis_od", "sphere_os", "cylinder_os",
              "axis_os", "add_power", "issued_by_name", "issued_at",
              "valid_from", "valid_until", "status"],

}
```

```json
{

  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "task_id": "EXP202610310001",
    "status": "QUEUED",
    "estimated_rows": 2910,
    "poll_url": "/api/v1/m10/prescriptions/export/EXP202610310001"
  },
  "trace_id": "a1b2c3d4e5f6a7b8"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 幂等键；2) 校验 `m10:rx:export`；3) 日期跨度 > 366 天 → `"1002032"`；4) 先 `COUNT(*)` 预估行数，> 50000 → `"1002031"`；5) 投递 Celery 任务 `export_prescriptions_task`，队列不可用 → `"1002030"`；6) 返回 `task_id` 与轮询地址；7) 导出内容**不含任何金额字段**（价格归 M16-04）。

```json
    "expire_reason": "EXPIRED",
    "expire_error_code": "1002009",
    "expire_message": "处方已过期，请复验后重新开具",
    "lens_type": "SINGLE_VISION",
    "eyes": [
      { "eye_side": "OD", "sphere": -4.25, "cylinder": -0.50, "axis": 10,
        "va_cc": 1.00, "pd": 32.00, "ph": null }
    ],
    "supply_blocked": false
  },
  "trace_id": "a1b2c3d4e5f6a7b8"
}
```

**响应（处方不存在）**

```json
{
  "code": "1002001",
  "message": "处方不存在或已删除：RX202610310099",
  "data": null,
  "trace_id": "a1b2c3d4e5f6a7b8"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 内部服务鉴权（服务间 token，不走用户权限）；2) 按 `rx_no` 查主表，不存在或 `is_deleted` → `"1002001"`；3) `selectinload` 取明细；4) 计算 `is_valid`（`status='AUDITED'` 且今天在 `[valid_from, valid_until]`）；5) **过期不抛错**，返回 `is_valid=false` + `expire_reason='EXPIRED'` + `expire_error_code="1002009"`，由下游决定拒绝下单并透传该错误码；6) `supply_blocked` 取最近一次可供性校验的 `is_blocking`；7) 返回 `sign_hash` 供下游校验处方未被篡改；8) 记录调用日志（`action='VIEW_EXTERNAL'` 仅在人工调阅时写，程序调用只打日志表外的访问日志）；9) SLA P95 ≤ 100ms（走 `uk_m10_prescription_no` 单点查询）。

#### API-31 P-06 取患者有效处方（内部契约）

**请求**

```
GET /internal/v1/m10/prescriptions/valid/by-patient/10021?lens_type=SINGLE_VISION
```

**响应（有有效处方）**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "patient_id": 10021,
    "patient_name": "李某某",
    "age": 10,
    "age_group": "CHILD",
    "has_valid_rx": true,
    "rx_count": 1,
    "nearest_expire_date": "2027-04-30",
    "supply_blocked": false,
    "latest": {
      "rx_no": "RX202610310012",
      "status": "AUDITED",
      "is_valid": true,
      "valid_from": "2026-10-31",
      "valid_until": "2027-04-30",
      "days_to_expire": 181,
      "lens_type": "SINGLE_VISION",
      "lens_type_label": "单光",
      "refractive_index_req": 1.60,
      "pd_total": 64.00,
      "issued_by_name": "张医师",
      "sign_hash": "a7f3c1e9b60d4a8f2c5e7b1d3f9a0c2e4b6d8f1a3c5e7b9d0f2a4c6e8b0d1f39"
    },
    "items": [
      { "eye_side": "OD", "sphere": -4.75, "cylinder": -0.75, "axis": 180,
        "add_power": null, "va_cc": 1.00, "pd": 32.50, "ph": 22.50 },
      { "eye_side": "OS", "sphere": -4.50, "cylinder": -1.00, "axis": 175,
        "add_power": null, "va_cc": 1.00, "pd": 31.50, "ph": 22.00 }
    ]
  },
  "trace_id": "a1b2c3d4e5f6a7b8"
}
```

**响应（无有效处方，但有最近一张过期处方供参考）**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "patient_id": 10021,
    "patient_name": "李某某",
    "age": 10,
    "age_group": "CHILD",
    "has_valid_rx": false,
    "rx_count": 0,
    "nearest_expire_date": null,
    "supply_blocked": false,
    "latest": {
      "rx_no": "RX202510310003",
      "status": "EXPIRED",
      "is_valid": false,
      "valid_from": "2025-10-31",
      "valid_until": "2026-04-30",
      "days_to_expire": -184,
      "expire_reason": "EXPIRED",
      "expire_error_code": "1002009"
    },
    "items": []
  },
  "trace_id": "a1b2c3d4e5f6a7b8"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 内部服务鉴权；2) 走部分索引 `idx_m10_rx_valid`（`status='AUDITED' AND is_deleted=FALSE`）按 `patient_id` + `valid_until` 取有效处方；3) 可选的 `lens_type` 过滤（M10-05 选离焦镜时只要离焦处方）；4) `latest` 取 `issued_at DESC` 的第一张；`items` 为最新有效处方的明细；5) 无有效处方时回退返回最近一张（含 `EXPIRED`）供 M10-05 展示与提示「需复验」；6) `rx_count` 为有效处方张数（同一患者可能同时有离焦与单光两张）；7) `supply_blocked` 取最近有效处方的可供性阻断标记；8) SLA P95 ≤ 100ms。

#### API-32 P-07 处方审核结果回调（内部契约）

**请求**

```
POST /internal/v1/m10/prescriptions/on-audited
```

```json
{
  "rx_no": "RX202610310011",
  "audit_result": "PASS",
  "audit_no": "RXA2026103100013",
  "auditor_id": 6002,
  "audited_at": "2026-10-31T09:35:02+08:00",
  "note": "M16-04 下单前复核通过",
  "callback_source": "M16-04"
}
```

**响应（首次接收）**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "rx_no": "RX202610310011",
    "accepted": true,
    "idempotent": false,
    "status": "AUDITED",
    "valid_until": "2027-10-31",
    "supply_blocked": false
  },
  "trace_id": "a1b2c3d4e5f6a7b8"
}
```

**响应（重复回调 — 幂等）**

```json
{
  "code": 0,
  "message": "success",
  "data": {
    "rx_no": "RX202610310011",
    "accepted": false,
    "idempotent": true,
    "status": "AUDITED",
    "valid_until": "2027-10-31",
    "supply_blocked": false,
    "note": "该审核结果已处理（audit_no=RXA2026103100013），本次为重复回调"
  },
  "trace_id": "a1b2c3d4e5f6a7b8"
}
```

**业务逻辑：** 1) 内部服务鉴权（`callback_source` 校验）；2) 按 `rx_no` 取处方，不存在 → `"1002001"`；3) 幂等：按 `(rx_no, audit_no, audit_result)` 查 `m10_prescription_audit`，已存在则 `accepted=false, idempotent=true` 直接返回当前状态；4) 若处方本地审核已完成（`status='AUDITED'`）且回调结论一致，同样视为幂等；5) 结论不一致（本地 `AUDITED` 但回调 `REJECT`）→ 不覆盖本地结论，返回 `accepted=false` 并置 `conflict=true` 提示人工核查（写 `WARNING` 日志）；6) 回调 `PASS`/`PASS_WITH_NOTE` 时：新增一条 `audit_type='EXTERNAL'` 的审核记录、置 `AUDITED`、签发、发布 `m10.prescription.issued` 事件；7) 回调 `REJECT` 时置 `REJECTED` 并写 `reject_reason`（取 `note`）；8) 返回当前 `valid_until` 与 `supply_blocked`，让 M16-04 可直接决定是否允许下单；9) SLA P95 ≤ 100ms。

### 6.4 FastAPI 路由实现示例

```python
# app/api/v1/m10/prescription.py
"""M10-02 配镜处方与处方审核 —— 路由层"""
from typing import List, Optional

from fastapi import APIRouter, Body, Depends, Header, Path, Query, Request
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession

from app.core.database import get_db
from app.core.response import PageResult, success_response
from app.core.security import get_current_user, require_permission
from app.schemas.m10.prescription import (
    RxAuditIn,
    RxConfigUpdateIn,
    RxCreateIn,
    RxOverridePassIn,
    RxQueryIn,
    RxReissueIn,
    RxRenewIn,
    RxRuleIn,
    RxSubmitAuditIn,
    RxSupplyCheckIn,
    RxTemplateIn,
    RxUpdateIn,
    RxVoidIn,
)
from app.services.m10 import prescription_service as svc
from app.services.m10 import prescription_audit_service as audit_svc

router = APIRouter(prefix="/api/v1/m10/prescriptions", tags=["M10-02 配镜处方"])
internal_router = APIRouter(prefix="/internal/v1/m10/prescriptions", tags=["M10-02 内部契约"])


# ==================== ① 主页与统计 ====================

@router.get("/workbench", summary="配镜处方工作台")
async def workbench(
    dept_id: Optional[int] = Query(None),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m10:rx:view")),
):
    """统计卡 + 待办提醒 + 今日我的处方，缓存 60 秒。"""
    data = await svc.build_workbench(db, user=user, dept_id=dept_id)
    return success_response(data)


@router.get("/statistics", summary="处方统计")
async def statistics(
    date_from: str = Query(..., pattern=r"^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$"),
    date_to: str = Query(..., pattern=r"^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$"),
    org_id: Optional[int] = Query(None),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m10:rx:stat")),
):
    """期间开方量、来源/镜片/医师分布、到期预警。"""
    data = await svc.build_statistics(db, user=user, date_from=date_from,
                                      date_to=date_to, org_id=org_id)
    return success_response(data)


# ==================== ② 列表与详情 ====================

@router.get("", summary="处方分页列表")
async def list_rx(
    query: RxQueryIn = Depends(),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m10:rx:view")),
):
    """按状态/来源/镜片类型/日期等筛选，支持仅查有效处方。"""
    items, total = await svc.list_prescriptions(db, user=user, query=query)
    page = PageResult(items=items, total=total, page=query.page,
                      page_size=query.page_size)
    return success_response(page)


@router.get("/{rx_no}", summary="处方详情")
async def get_rx(
    rx_no: str = Path(..., max_length=32),
    include_chain: bool = Query(False),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m10:rx:view")),
):
    """主表 + 明细 + 审核轨迹 + 可供性轨迹（可选处方链）。"""
    data = await svc.get_detail(db, user=user, rx_no=rx_no, include_chain=include_chain)
    return success_response(data)


@router.get("/by-patient/{patient_id}", summary="按患者查处方历史")
async def list_by_patient(
    patient_id: int = Path(...),
    include_expired: bool = Query(True),
    page: int = Query(1, ge=1),
    page_size: int = Query(10, ge=1, le=100),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m10:rx:view")),
):
    """患者历史处方与球镜序列（处方口径）。"""
    items, total = await svc.list_by_patient(db, user=user, patient_id=patient_id,
                                             include_expired=include_expired,
                                             page=page, page_size=page_size)
    page_result = PageResult(items=items, total=total, page=page, page_size=page_size)
    return success_response(page_result)


@router.get("/pending-audit", summary="待审核队列")
async def pending_audit(
    dept_id: Optional[int] = Query(None),
    only_mine: bool = Query(False),
    page: int = Query(1, ge=1),
    page_size: int = Query(50, ge=1, le=200),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m10:rx:audit")),
):
    """按提交时间升序，超时标红，儿童首配标双人复核。"""
    data = await audit_svc.list_pending(db, user=user, dept_id=dept_id,
                                        only_mine=only_mine, page=page,
                                        page_size=page_size)
    return success_response(data)


# ==================== ③ 开立 ====================

@router.post("", summary="新建处方（草稿）")
async def create_rx(
    payload: RxCreateIn,
    idempotency_key: Optional[str] = Header(None, alias="Idempotency-Key"),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m10:rx:create")),
):
    """支持 Idempotency-Key；计算偏差/球镜等效/有效期，落库为 DRAFT。"""
    data = await svc.create_prescription(db, user=user, payload=payload,
                                        idempotency_key=idempotency_key)
    return success_response(data)


@router.put("/{rx_no}", summary="修改处方")
async def update_rx(
    rx_no: str = Path(..., max_length=32),
    payload: RxUpdateIn = Body(...),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m10:rx:update")),
):
    """仅 DRAFT / REJECTED 可改；version 乐观锁。"""
    data = await svc.update_prescription(db, user=user, rx_no=rx_no, payload=payload)
    return success_response(data)


@router.delete("/{rx_no}", summary="删除草稿")
async def delete_rx(
    rx_no: str = Path(..., max_length=32),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m10:rx:delete")),
):
    """逻辑删除草稿及其明细。"""
    data = await svc.delete_draft(db, user=user, rx_no=rx_no)
    return success_response(data)


@router.post("/precheck", summary="审核预检")
async def precheck(
    payload: dict = Body(...),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m10:rx:create")),
):
    """规则引擎预检，不落库，返回 error/warn/info 命中明细。"""
    data = await audit_svc.precheck(db, user=user, payload=payload)
    return success_response(data)


@router.post("/{rx_no}/submit-audit", summary="提交审核")
async def submit_audit(
    rx_no: str = Path(..., max_length=32),
    payload: RxSubmitAuditIn = Body(...),
    idempotency_key: Optional[str] = Header(None, alias="Idempotency-Key"),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m10:rx:update")),
):
    """生成处方号，创建待审记录，状态置 PENDING_AUDIT。"""
    data = await svc.submit_audit(db, user=user, rx_no=rx_no, payload=payload,
                                 idempotency_key=idempotency_key)
    return success_response(data)


# ==================== ④ 审核 ====================

@router.post("/{rx_no}/audit", summary="审核")
async def audit_rx(
    rx_no: str = Path(..., max_length=32),
    payload: RxAuditIn = Body(...),
    idempotency_key: Optional[str] = Header(None, alias="Idempotency-Key"),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m10:rx:audit")),
):
    """通过 / 带说明通过 / 驳回；儿童首配走双人复核。"""
    data = await audit_svc.audit(db, user=user, rx_no=rx_no, payload=payload,
                                 idempotency_key=idempotency_key)
    return success_response(data)


@router.post("/{rx_no}/override-pass", summary="越权放行")
async def override_pass(
    rx_no: str = Path(..., max_length=32),
    payload: RxOverridePassIn = Body(...),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m10:rx:approve")),
):
    """存在 ERROR 命中时，凭 M20 审批单强制通过，标记质控。"""
    data = await audit_svc.override_pass(db, user=user, rx_no=rx_no, payload=payload)
    return success_response(data)


@router.post("/{rx_no}/withdraw", summary="撤回审核")
async def withdraw_audit(
    rx_no: str = Path(..., max_length=32),
    payload: dict = Body(...),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m10:rx:update")),
):
    """首轮未出结论时可撤回，回到 DRAFT（处方号保留）。"""
    data = await svc.withdraw_audit(db, user=user, rx_no=rx_no, payload=payload)
    return success_response(data)


# ==================== ⑤ 状态流转 ====================

@router.post("/{rx_no}/void", summary="作废处方")
async def void_rx(
    rx_no: str = Path(..., max_length=32),
    payload: RxVoidIn = Body(...),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m10:rx:update")),
):
    """原因 ≥ 10 字；已被订单引用时报 1002027。"""
    data = await svc.void_prescription(db, user=user, rx_no=rx_no, payload=payload)
    return success_response(data)


@router.post("/{rx_no}/renew", summary="复验换发")
async def renew_rx(
    rx_no: str = Path(..., max_length=32),
    payload: RxRenewIn = Body(...),
    idempotency_key: Optional[str] = Header(None, alias="Idempotency-Key"),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m10:rx:create")),
):
    """基于新验光单生成新处方，维护处方链。"""
    data = await svc.renew_prescription(db, user=user, rx_no=rx_no, payload=payload,
                                        idempotency_key=idempotency_key)
    return success_response(data)


@router.post("/{rx_no}/reissue", summary="补开处方")
async def reissue_rx(
    rx_no: str = Path(..., max_length=32),
    payload: RxReissueIn = Body(...),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m10:rx:create")),
):
    """内容不变重新出具，原过期时打印件加「已过期」水印。"""
    data = await svc.reissue_prescription(db, user=user, rx_no=rx_no, payload=payload)
    return success_response(data)


# ==================== ⑥ 打印与签署 ====================

@router.post("/{rx_no}/print", summary="签署并打印")
async def print_rx(
    rx_no: str = Path(..., max_length=32),
    payload: dict = Body(...),
    request: Request = None,
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m10:rx:print")),
):
    """校验签名摘要、份数上限，渲染 A5 处方笺（不含金额）。"""
    data = await svc.sign_and_print(db, user=user, rx_no=rx_no, payload=payload,
                                    client_ip=request.client.host if request else None)
    return success_response(data)


# ==================== ⑦ 模板 ====================

@router.get("/templates", summary="模板列表")
async def list_templates(
    owner_scope: Optional[str] = Query(None),
    template_kind: Optional[str] = Query(None),
    lens_type: Optional[str] = Query(None),
    page: int = Query(1, ge=1),
    page_size: int = Query(20, ge=1, le=100),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m10:rx:view")),
):
    """个人/科室/全院三级可见性，默认按使用次数排序。"""
    items, total = await svc.list_templates(db, user=user, owner_scope=owner_scope,
                                            template_kind=template_kind,
                                            lens_type=lens_type, page=page,
                                            page_size=page_size)
    page_result = PageResult(items=items, total=total, page=page, page_size=page_size)
    return success_response(page_result)


@router.post("/templates", summary="新建模板")
async def create_template(
    payload: RxTemplateIn,
    idempotency_key: Optional[str] = Header(None, alias="Idempotency-Key"),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m10:rx:create")),
):
    """生成 RXT 编码；成套模板必须有明细。"""
    data = await svc.create_template(db, user=user, payload=payload,
                                     idempotency_key=idempotency_key)
    return success_response(data)


@router.put("/templates/{tpl_id}", summary="修改模板")
async def update_template(
    tpl_id: int = Path(...),
    payload: RxTemplateIn = Body(...),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m10:rx:update")),
):
    """个人模板仅归属人可改；version 乐观锁。"""
    data = await svc.update_template(db, user=user, tpl_id=tpl_id, payload=payload)
    return success_response(data)


@router.patch("/templates/{tpl_id}/status", summary="启停模板")
async def set_template_status(
    tpl_id: int = Path(...),
    payload: dict = Body(...),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m10:rx:update")),
):
    """停用不删除，历史引用仍可读。"""
    data = await svc.set_template_status(db, user=user, tpl_id=tpl_id, payload=payload)
    return success_response(data)


# ==================== ⑧ 可供性校验 ====================

@router.post("/{rx_no}/supply-check", summary="处方光度可供性校验")
async def supply_check(
    rx_no: str = Path(..., max_length=32),
    payload: RxSupplyCheckIn = Body(default_factory=RxSupplyCheckIn),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m10:rx:view")),
):
    """转调 M01-08 specs/lookup，落快照并回写主表/明细状态。"""
    data = await svc.check_supply(db, user=user, rx_no=rx_no, payload=payload)
    return success_response(data)


@router.get("/{rx_no}/supply-check/latest", summary="最近一次可供性校验结果")
async def latest_supply_check(
    rx_no: str = Path(..., max_length=32),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m10:rx:view")),
):
    """含是否过期（stale）与 M01-08 版本是否变化的标记。"""
    data = await svc.get_latest_supply_check(db, user=user, rx_no=rx_no)
    return success_response(data)


# ==================== ⑨ 规则与配置 ====================

@router.get("/audit-rules", summary="审核规则列表")
async def list_rules(
    status: Optional[str] = Query(None),
    rule_category: Optional[str] = Query(None),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m10:rx:config")),
):
    """32 条配置驱动规则，按 priority 升序。"""
    data = await audit_svc.list_rules(db, user=user, status=status,
                                      rule_category=rule_category)
    return success_response(data)


@router.put("/audit-rules/{rule_code}", summary="修改审核规则")
async def update_rule(
    rule_code: str = Path(..., pattern=r"^RAR\d{3}$"),
    payload: RxRuleIn = Body(...),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m10:rx:config")),
):
    """ERROR 级规则需 m10:rx:approve；bump version_tag 并失效缓存。"""
    data = await audit_svc.upsert_rule(db, user=user, rule_code=rule_code, payload=payload)
    return success_response(data)


@router.get("/configs", summary="系统参数列表")
async def list_configs(
    config_group: Optional[str] = Query(None),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m10:rx:config")),
):
    """25 项参数，敏感项脱敏。"""
    data = await svc.list_configs(db, user=user, config_group=config_group)
    return success_response(data)


@router.put("/configs", summary="修改系统参数")
async def update_configs(
    items: List[RxConfigUpdateIn] = Body(...),
    approval_no: Optional[str] = Body(None),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m10:rx:config")),
):
    """校验区间与类型；敏感参数需审批单号。"""
    data = await svc.update_configs(db, user=user, items=items, approval_no=approval_no)
    return success_response(data)


# ==================== ⑩ 导出 ====================

@router.post("/export", summary="导出处方清单")
async def export_rx(
    payload: dict = Body(...),
    idempotency_key: Optional[str] = Header(None, alias="Idempotency-Key"),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
    user=Depends(require_permission("m10:rx:export")),
):
    """异步导出，上限 50000 行，不含金额字段。"""
    data = await svc.export_prescriptions(db, user=user, payload=payload,
                                          idempotency_key=idempotency_key)
    return success_response(data)


# ==================== ⑪ 内部契约（提供给其他模块） ====================

@internal_router.get("/{rx_no}", summary="P-05 取配镜处方")
async def p05_get_prescription(
    rx_no: str = Path(..., max_length=32),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
    caller: str = Depends(verify_internal_token),
):
    """M16-04 / M10-06 / M16-06 取处方；过期返回 200 + is_valid=false。"""
    data = await svc.internal_get(db, caller=caller, rx_no=rx_no)
    return success_response(data)


@internal_router.get("/valid/by-patient/{patient_id}", summary="P-06 患者有效处方")
async def p06_valid_prescriptions(
    patient_id: int = Path(...),
    lens_type: Optional[str] = Query(None),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
    caller: str = Depends(verify_internal_token),
):
    """M10-05 / M16-04 取患者有效处方；无有效时回退最近一张、取数供提示。"""
    data = await svc.internal_valid_by_patient(db, caller=caller,
                                               patient_id=patient_id,
                                               lens_type=lens_type)
    return success_response(data)


@internal_router.post("/on-audited", summary="P-07 处方审核结果回调")
async def p07_on_audited(
    payload: dict = Body(...),
    db: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
    caller: str = Depends(verify_internal_token),
):
    """M16-04 下单前校验后回写审核结论；按 (rx_no, audit_no) 幂等。"""
    data = await audit_svc.on_audited_callback(db, caller=caller, payload=payload)
    return success_response(data)
```

### 6.5 Service 层核心逻辑示例

```python
# app/services/m10/prescription_service.py
"""M10-02 配镜处方与处方审核 —— 核心服务"""
import hashlib
import json
from datetime import date, datetime, timedelta
from decimal import ROUND_HALF_UP, Decimal
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional, Tuple

from sqlalchemy import func, select
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession
from sqlalchemy.orm import selectinload

from app.clients.m01_optical_client import OpticalClient
from app.clients.m03_patient_client import PatientClient
from app.core.errors import BizError
from app.core.redis import RedisHelper
from app.models.m10.prescription import (
    Prescription,
    PrescriptionItem,
    PrescriptionLog,
    PrescriptionSupplyCheck,
)
from app.schemas.m10.prescription import RxCreateIn, RxUpdateIn

D = Decimal
Q025 = D("0.25")
Q001 = D("0.01")


class PrescriptionService:
    """配镜处方的开立、有效期、可供性与内部契约实现。"""

    def __init__(self, optical: Optional[OpticalClient] = None,
                 patient: Optional[PatientClient] = None) -> None:
        self.optical = optical or OpticalClient()
        self.patient = patient or PatientClient()

    # ---------------------------------------------------------------- 工具方法

    @staticmethod
    def round_half_up(value: Decimal, quant: Decimal = Q001) -> Decimal:
        """ROUND_HALF_UP 舍入（PostgreSQL 与临床口径一致）。"""
        return D(str(value)).quantize(quant, rounding=ROUND_HALF_UP)

    @staticmethod
    def calc_age_group(age: Optional[int], cfg: Dict[str, Any]) -> str:
        """按年龄分组：儿童 ≤12、成人 13~59、老年 ≥60（阈值可配置）。"""
        if age is None:
            return "ADULT"
        child_max = int(cfg.get("M10.RX.CHILD_AGE_MAX", 12))
        senior_min = int(cfg.get("M10.RX.SENIOR_AGE_MIN", 60))
        if age <= child_max:
            return "CHILD"
        if age >= senior_min:
            return "SENIOR"
        return "ADULT"

    @staticmethod
    def calc_validity(age_group: str, cfg: Dict[str, Any],
                      explicit_months: Optional[int] = None) -> Tuple[int, str]:
        """返回 (有效月数, 起算依据)。儿童 6 个月、成人/老年 12 个月。"""
        if explicit_months is not None:
            if explicit_months < 1 or explicit_months > 36:
                raise BizError("1002022", "处方有效期必须为 1~36 个月")
            return explicit_months, "CONFIG_DEFAULT"
        key = {
            "CHILD": "M10.RX.VALIDITY_MONTHS_CHILD",
            "ADULT": "M10.RX.VALIDITY_MONTHS_ADULT",
            "SENIOR": "M10.RX.VALIDITY_MONTHS_SENIOR",
        }[age_group]
        default_months = {"CHILD": 6, "ADULT": 12, "SENIOR": 12}[age_group]
        return int(cfg.get(key, default_months)), "CONFIG_DEFAULT"

    @staticmethod
    def add_months(basis: date, months: int) -> date:
        """加 N 个月并对齐月末（2026-10-31 + 6 月 = 2027-04-30）。"""
        total = basis.month - 1 + months
        year = basis.year + total // 12
        month = total % 12 + 1
        days = [31, 29 if (year % 4 == 0 and (year % 100 != 0 or year % 400 == 0)) else 28,
                31, 30, 31, 30, 31, 31, 30, 31, 30, 31]
        day = min(basis.day, days[month - 1])
        return date(year, month, day)

    @staticmethod
    def calc_se(sphere: Decimal, cylinder: Decimal) -> Decimal:
        """球镜等效 = 球镜 + 柱镜 / 2，保留两位小数。"""
        return (D(str(sphere)) + D(str(cylinder)) / D(2)).quantize(Q001, rounding=ROUND_HALF_UP)

    @staticmethod
    def calc_axis_deviation(axis_rx: Optional[int], axis_ref: Optional[int]) -> Optional[int]:
        """轴位循环差值，结果恒为 0~90。"""
        if axis_rx is None or axis_ref is None:
            return None
        diff = abs(axis_rx - axis_ref) % 180
        return diff if diff <= 90 else 180 - diff

    @staticmethod
    def build_sign_hash(payload: Dict[str, Any]) -> str:
        """规范化 JSON 后取 SHA-256，作为处方防篡改摘要。"""
        raw = json.dumps(payload, ensure_ascii=False, sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":"))
        return hashlib.sha256(raw.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()

    # ---------------------------------------------------------------- 数值换算

    @staticmethod
    def normalize_cylinder(sphere: Decimal, cylinder: Decimal,
                           axis: Optional[int]) -> Tuple[Decimal, Decimal, Optional[int]]:
        """正柱镜表述自动换算为负柱镜：S'=S+C, C'=-C, A'=A±90。"""
        sph, cyl = D(str(sphere)), D(str(cylinder))
        if cyl > 0:
            new_axis = None if axis is None else (axis + 90 if axis <= 90 else axis - 90)
            return sph + cyl, -cyl, new_axis
        return sph, cyl, axis

    # ---------------------------------------------------------------- 新建处方

    async def create_prescription(self, db: AsyncSession, user, payload: RxCreateIn,
                                  idempotency_key: Optional[str] = None) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """新建草稿：校验来源、计算偏差与有效期、落库主表与明细。"""
        cache_key = "m10:idem:rx:create:%s" % idempotency_key
        if idempotency_key:
            cached = await RedisHelper.get_json(cache_key)
            if cached:
                return cached

        cfg = await self._load_config(db, user.org_id)

        # 1) 患者快照
        patient = await self.patient.get_brief(payload.patient_id)
        age_group = self.calc_age_group(patient.get("age"), cfg)
        is_minor = (patient.get("age") or 99) < int(cfg.get("M10.RX.MINOR_AGE_MAX", 18))

        # 2) 来源校验
        optometry_no = payload.optometry_no
        optometry_id = None
        external_rx_date = None
        if payload.rx_source == "OPTOMETRY":
            if not optometry_no:
                raise BizError("1002002", "来源为本院验光单时必须提供验光单号")
            optometry = await self._fetch_optometry(optometry_no)
            optometry_id = optometry["id"]
            pupil_dilated = bool(optometry.get("pupil_dilated"))
        elif payload.rx_source == "EXTERNAL":
            if not (payload.external_hospital and payload.external_rx_no
                    and payload.external_rx_date):
                raise BizError("1002025", "外院处方必须填写外院名称、外院处方号与开单日期")
            external_rx_date = payload.external_rx_date
            pupil_dilated = False
        else:
            pupil_dilated = False

        # 3) 未成年人必须有监护人
        if is_minor and not payload.guardian_name:
            raise BizError("1002005", "未成年人配镜处方必须填写监护人信息")

        # 4) 偏差与球镜等效
        items, devices = [], {}
        for item in payload.items:
            sph, cyl, axis = self.normalize_cylinder(item.sphere, item.cylinder, item.axis)
            dev_sph = dev_cyl = dev_axis = None
            ref = (optometry.get("eyes", {}) or {}).get(item.eye_side) if optometry_no else None
            if ref:
                dev_sph = self.round_half_up(sph - D(str(ref["sphere"])))
                dev_cyl = self.round_half_up(cyl - D(str(ref["cylinder"])))
                dev_axis = self.calc_axis_deviation(axis, ref.get("axis"))
            devices[item.eye_side] = {
                "sphere": sph, "cylinder": cyl, "axis": axis,
                "deviation_sph": dev_sph, "deviation_cyl": dev_cyl,
                "deviation_axis": dev_axis, "sphere_equivalent": self.calc_se(sph, cyl),
            }
            items.append({"eye_side": item.eye_side, "sphere": sph, "cylinder": cyl,
                          "axis": axis, "add_power": item.add_power,
                          "va_sc": item.va_sc, "va_cc": item.va_cc,
                          "pd": item.pd, "ph": item.ph,
                          "refractive_index": item.refractive_index,
                          "lens_material": item.lens_material})

        max_sph = max([abs(v["deviation_sph"]) for v in devices.values()
                       if v["deviation_sph"] is not None], default=D("0"))
        max_cyl = max([abs(v["deviation_cyl"]) for v in devices.values()
                       if v["deviation_cyl"] is not None], default=D("0"))
        thr_sph = D(str(cfg.get("M10.RX.DEVIATION_SPH_MAX", "0.50")))
        thr_cyl = D(str(cfg.get("M10.RX.DEVIATION_CYL_MAX", "0.50")))
        if (max_sph > thr_sph or max_cyl > thr_cyl) and \
                not (payload.deviation_note and len(payload.deviation_note.strip()) >= 10):
            raise BizError("1002007",
                           "处方与验光偏差超出阈值（球镜 %sD），请填写临床调整原因与偏差说明"
                           % max_sph)

        # 5) 有效期
        months, basis_kind = self.calc_validity(age_group, cfg, payload.validity_months)
        basis = external_rx_date or date.today()
        if external_rx_date:
            basis_kind = "EXTERNAL_RX_DATE"
            remain_days = (self.add_months(basis, months) - date.today()).days
            if remain_days < 0:
                raise BizError("1002009", "外院处方已超过有效期，请在本院复验后开立")

        # 6) 落库
        rx = Prescription(
            patient_id=payload.patient_id,
            patient_name=patient["patient_name"],
            gender=patient.get("gender", "U"),
            age=patient.get("age"),
            age_group=age_group,
            is_minor=is_minor,
            guardian_name=payload.guardian_name,
            rx_source=payload.rx_source,
            optometry_id=optometry_id,
            optometry_no=optometry_no,
            lens_type=payload.lens_type,
            lens_type_label={"SINGLE_VISION": "单光", "PROGRESSIVE": "渐进多焦",
                             "BIFOCAL": "双光", "DEFOCUS": "离焦",
                             "PRISM": "棱镜"}.get(payload.lens_type, payload.lens_type),
            pd_total=payload.pd_total,
            pd_right=payload.pd_right,
            pd_left=payload.pd_left,
            pd_near=payload.pd_near,
            validity_months=months,
            valid_from=basis,
            valid_until=self.add_months(basis, months),
            expiry_basis=basis_kind,
            status="DRAFT",
            audit_required=True,
            deviation_note=payload.deviation_note,
            deviation_max_sph=max_sph,
            deviation_max_cyl=max_cyl,
            clinical_reason=payload.clinical_reason,
            org_id=user.org_id,
            created_by=user.id,
        )
        db.add(rx)
        await db.flush()

        for raw in items:
            dev = devices[raw["eye_side"]]
            db.add(PrescriptionItem(
                rx_id=rx.id, eye_side=raw["eye_side"], sphere=raw["sphere"],
                cylinder=raw["cylinder"], axis=raw["axis"],
                add_power=raw["add_power"], sphere_equivalent=dev["sphere_equivalent"],
                va_sc=raw["va_sc"], va_cc=raw["va_cc"], pd=raw["pd"], ph=raw["ph"],
                refractive_index=raw["refractive_index"],
                deviation_sph=dev["deviation_sph"], deviation_cyl=dev["deviation_cyl"],
                deviation_axis=dev["deviation_axis"],
                is_manual_input=False,
            ))

        await self._write_log(db, rx, action="CREATE", from_status=None, to_status="DRAFT",
                              operator=user, summary="新建处方草稿")
        await db.commit()

        result = {
            "id": rx.id, "rx_no": None, "status": "DRAFT", "status_label": "草稿",
            "valid_from": rx.valid_from.isoformat(),
            "valid_until": rx.valid_until.isoformat(),
            "validity_months": months, "age_group": age_group,
            "deviation_max_sph": float(max_sph), "deviation_max_cyl": float(max_cyl),
            "sphere_equivalent": {k: float(v["sphere_equivalent"])
                                  for k, v in devices.items()},
            "version": 0, "next_step": "SUPPLY_CHECK",
        }
        if idempotency_key:
            await RedisHelper.set_json(cache_key, result, ttl=86400)
        return result

    # ---------------------------------------------------------------- 提交审核

    async def submit_audit(self, db: AsyncSession, user, rx_no: str,
                           payload, idempotency_key: Optional[str] = None) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """DRAFT/REJECTED → PENDING_AUDIT，生成处方号。"""
        rx = await self._get_or_raise(db, rx_no, with_items=True)
        if rx.status not in ("DRAFT", "REJECTED"):
            raise BizError("1002006", "当前状态（%s）不允许提交审核" % rx.status)
        if rx.version != payload.version:
            raise BizError("40900", "处方已被他人修改，请刷新后重试")
        if await self._audit_has_error(db, rx):
            raise BizError("1002038", "预检存在错误级规则命中，请先修正")
        if rx.supply_check_status == "NOT_CHECKED":
            raise BizError("1002021", "提交审核前必须先完成处方光度可供性校验")

        if not rx.rx_no:
            rx.rx_no = await self._next_rx_no(db, date.today())
        rx.status = "PENDING_AUDIT"
        rx.audit_round = (rx.audit_round or 0) + 1
        rx.issued_by = user.id
        rx.issued_by_name = user.name
        rx.issued_dept_id = user.dept_id
        rx.issued_dept_name = user.dept_name
        rx.version += 1
        await self._create_pending_audit(db, rx, payload)
        await self._write_log(db, rx, action="SUBMIT_AUDIT", from_status="DRAFT",
                              to_status="PENDING_AUDIT", operator=user,
                              summary="提交审核，第 %d 轮" % rx.audit_round)
        await db.commit()
        return {"rx_no": rx.rx_no, "status": rx.status, "audit_round": rx.audit_round,
                "version": rx.version}

    # ---------------------------------------------------------------- 可供性校验

    async def check_supply(self, db: AsyncSession, user, rx_no: str,
                           payload) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """调 M01-08 specs/lookup 校验光度可供，落快照并回写状态。"""
        rx = await self._get_or_raise(db, rx_no, with_items=True)
        cfg = await self._load_config(db, rx.org_id)
        block = str(cfg.get("M10.RX.SUPPLY_BLOCK_ON_UNAVAILABLE", "TRUE")) == "TRUE"
        eyes = payload.eye_sides or ["OD", "OS"]
        check_no = await self._next_check_no(db, date.today())
        ok_count, blocked_eyes, degraded = 0, [], False
        checked_eyes: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []

        version = await self.optical.get_version()
        for item in [i for i in rx.items if i.eye_side in eyes]:
            try:
                # 上游契约：POST /internal/v1/optical/specs/lookup
                res = await self.optical.spec_lookup(
                    product_code=payload.product_code,
                    sphere=float(item.sphere), cylinder=float(item.cylinder),
                    axis=item.axis, add_power=float(item.add_power) if item.add_power else None,
                )
            except Exception:  # 上游不可用 → 降级，不阻断
                degraded = True
                db.add(PrescriptionSupplyCheck(
                    check_no=check_no, rx_id=rx.id, rx_no=rx.rx_no, rx_item_id=item.id,
                    eye_side=item.eye_side, sphere=item.sphere, cylinder=item.cylinder,
                    axis=item.axis, lookup_found=False, is_available=False,
                    unavailable_reason="SERVICE_ERROR", is_degraded=True,
                    check_source="RX_CREATE",
                ))
                item.supply_check_status = "CHECK_FAILED"
                checked_eyes.append({"eye_side": item.eye_side, "is_available": None,
                                     "is_degraded": True,
                                     "message": "光度校验服务暂不可用，请人工确认"})
                continue

            available = bool(res.get("is_available"))
            item.supply_check_status = "AVAILABLE" if available else "UNAVAILABLE"
            item.supply_type = res.get("supply_type")
            item.lead_time_days = res.get("lead_time_days")
            item.supply_message = res.get("message")
            if available:
                ok_count += 1
            else:
                blocked_eyes.append(item.eye_side)
            db.add(PrescriptionSupplyCheck(
                check_no=check_no, rx_id=rx.id, rx_no=rx.rx_no, rx_item_id=item.id,
                eye_side=item.eye_side, product_code=res.get("product_code"),
                sphere=item.sphere, cylinder=item.cylinder, axis=item.axis,
                lookup_found=bool(res.get("found")), is_available=available,
                supply_type=res.get("supply_type"),
                lead_time_days=res.get("lead_time_days"),
                final_price=res.get("final_price"),
                unavailable_reason=None if available else "OUT_OF_RANGE",
                is_blocking=(not available) and block, check_source="RX_CREATE",
                optical_version=version,
            ))
            checked_eyes.append({"eye_side": item.eye_side, "is_available": available,
                                 "supply_type": res.get("supply_type"),
                                 "lead_time_days": res.get("lead_time_days"),
                                 "message": res.get("message")})

        if degraded and ok_count == 0:
            status = "CHECK_FAILED"
        elif not blocked_eyes:
            status = "ALL_AVAILABLE"
        elif ok_count == 0:
            status = "NONE_AVAILABLE"
        else:
            status = "PARTIAL"

        rx.supply_check_status = status
        rx.supply_check_at = datetime.now()
        rx.supply_check_version = version
        rx.unavailable_eyes = ",".join(blocked_eyes) or None
        await self._write_log(db, rx, action="SUPPLY_CHECK", operator=user,
                              summary="可供性校验结果 %s" % status)
        await db.commit()
        return {"rx_no": rx.rx_no, "check_no": check_no, "supply_check_status": status,
                "supply_check_version": version, "is_degraded": degraded,
                "eyes": checked_eyes, "is_blocking": bool(blocked_eyes) and block}

    # ---------------------------------------------------------------- 内部契约

    async def internal_get(self, db: AsyncSession, caller: str, rx_no: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """P-05：取处方；过期返回 200 + is_valid=false（不抛错）。"""
        rx = await self._get_or_raise(db, rx_no, with_items=True)
        today = date.today()
        is_valid = rx.status == "AUDITED" and rx.valid_from <= today <= rx.valid_until
        payload = {
            "rx_no": rx.rx_no,
            "patient_id": rx.patient_id,
            "patient_name": rx.patient_name,
            "age": rx.age,
            "age_group": rx.age_group,
            "status": rx.status,
            "is_valid": is_valid,
            "valid_from": rx.valid_from.isoformat(),
            "valid_until": rx.valid_until.isoformat(),
            "days_to_expire": (rx.valid_until - today).days,
            "expire_reason": None if is_valid else self._expire_reason(rx, today),
            "lens_type": rx.lens_type,
            "lens_type_label": rx.lens_type_label,
            "pd_total": float(rx.pd_total) if rx.pd_total is not None else None,
            "issued_by": rx.issued_by,
            "issued_by_name": rx.issued_by_name,
            "sign_hash": rx.sign_hash,
            "supply_blocked": rx.supply_check_status in ("PARTIAL", "NONE_AVAILABLE"),
            "unavailable_eyes": rx.unavailable_eyes,
            "eyes": [
                {"eye_side": i.eye_side, "sphere": float(i.sphere),
                 "cylinder": float(i.cylinder), "axis": i.axis,
                 "add_power": float(i.add_power) if i.add_power is not None else None,
                 "va_cc": float(i.va_cc) if i.va_cc is not None else None,
                 "pd": float(i.pd) if i.pd is not None else None,
                 "ph": float(i.ph) if i.ph is not None else None,
                 "sphere_equivalent": float(i.sphere_equivalent)
                 if i.sphere_equivalent is not None else None}
                for i in sorted(rx.items, key=lambda x: x.eye_side) if not i.is_deleted
            ],
        }
        if not is_valid:
            payload["expire_error_code"] = "1002009"
            payload["expire_message"] = "处方已过期，请复验后重新开具"
        return payload

    async def internal_valid_by_patient(self, db: AsyncSession, caller: str,
                                        patient_id: int,
                                        lens_type: Optional[str] = None) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """P-06：取患者有效处方；无有效时回退最近一张供提示。"""
        stmt = (
            select(Prescription)
            .where(Prescription.patient_id == patient_id,
                   Prescription.status == "AUDITED",
                   Prescription.is_deleted.is_(False),
                   Prescription.valid_from <= date.today(),
                   Prescription.valid_until >= date.today())
            .options(selectinload(Prescription.items))
            .order_by(Prescription.issued_at.desc())
        )
        if lens_type:
            stmt = stmt.where(Prescription.lens_type == lens_type)
        rows = list((await db.execute(stmt)).scalars().all())

        if rows:
            latest = rows[0]
            items = [i for i in latest.items if not i.is_deleted]
            return {
                "patient_id": patient_id, "patient_name": latest.patient_name,
                "age": latest.age, "age_group": latest.age_group,
                "has_valid_rx": True, "rx_count": len(rows),
                "nearest_expire_date": min(r.valid_until for r in rows).isoformat(),
                "supply_blocked": latest.supply_check_status in ("PARTIAL", "NONE_AVAILABLE"),
                "latest": {"rx_no": latest.rx_no, "status": latest.status, "is_valid": True,
                           "valid_from": latest.valid_from.isoformat(),
                           "valid_until": latest.valid_until.isoformat(),
                           "days_to_expire": (latest.valid_until - date.today()).days,
                           "lens_type": latest.lens_type,
                           "sign_hash": latest.sign_hash},
                "items": [{"eye_side": i.eye_side, "sphere": float(i.sphere),
                           "cylinder": float(i.cylinder), "axis": i.axis,
                           "add_power": float(i.add_power) if i.add_power is not None else None,
                           "va_cc": float(i.va_cc) if i.va_cc is not None else None,
                           "pd": float(i.pd) if i.pd is not None else None,
                           "ph": float(i.ph) if i.ph is not None else None} for i in items],
            }

        # 无有效处方 → 回退最近一张（含过期），供 M10-05 提示「需复验」
        last = (await db.execute(
            select(Prescription)
            .where(Prescription.patient_id == patient_id,
                   Prescription.is_deleted.is_(False))
            .order_by(Prescription.issued_at.desc())
            .limit(1)
        )).scalars().first()
        return {
            "patient_id": patient_id,
            "patient_name": last.patient_name if last else None,
            "age": last.age if last else None,
            "age_group": last.age_group if last else None,
            "has_valid_rx": False, "rx_count": 0, "nearest_expire_date": None,
            "supply_blocked": False,
            "latest": None if last is None else {
                "rx_no": last.rx_no, "status": last.status, "is_valid": False,
                "valid_from": last.valid_from.isoformat(),
                "valid_until": last.valid_until.isoformat(),
                "days_to_expire": (last.valid_until - date.today()).days,
                "expire_reason": "EXPIRED", "expire_error_code": "1002009"},
            "items": [],
        }

    # ---------------------------------------------------------------- 私有助手

    @staticmethod
    def _expire_reason(rx: Prescription, today: date) -> Optional[str]:
        """判定不可用原因：EXPIRED / VOID / NOT_AUDITED。"""
        if rx.status == "EXPIRED" or rx.valid_until < today:
            return "EXPIRED"
        if rx.status == "VOID":
            return "VOID"
        if rx.status != "AUDITED":
            return "NOT_AUDITED"
        return None

    async def _get_or_raise(self, db: AsyncSession, rx_no: str,
                            with_items: bool = False) -> Prescription:
        """按处方号取主表，不存在报 1002001。"""
        stmt = select(Prescription).where(Prescription.rx_no == rx_no,
                                          Prescription.is_deleted.is_(False))
        if with_items:
            stmt = stmt.options(selectinload(Prescription.items))
        rx = (await db.execute(stmt)).scalars().first()
        if rx is None:
            raise BizError("1002001", "处方不存在或已删除：%s" % rx_no)
        return rx

    async def _next_rx_no(self, db: AsyncSession, day: date) -> str:
        """生成 RX+YYYYMMDD+4 位流水；Redis INCR，DB 唯一索引兜底。"""
        seq = await RedisHelper.incr("m10:code:RX:%s" % day.strftime("%Y%m%d"))
        if seq > 9999:
            raise BizError("1002017", "处方号生成失败（当日号段已满），请联系信息科")
        return "RX%s%04d" % (day.strftime("%Y%m%d"), seq)

    async def _next_check_no(self, db: AsyncSession, day: date) -> str:
        """生成 RSC+YYYYMMDD+6 位校验批次号。"""
        seq = await RedisHelper.incr("m10:code:RSC:%s" % day.strftime("%Y%m%d"))
        return "RSC%s%06d" % (day.strftime("%Y%m%d"), seq)

    async def _load_config(self, db: AsyncSession, org_id: int) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """加载处方参数（Redis 缓存 10 分钟）。"""
        cached = await RedisHelper.get_json("m10:rx:config:%d" % org_id)
        if cached:
            return cached
        from app.models.m10.prescription import PrescriptionConfig
        rows = (await db.execute(
            select(PrescriptionConfig).where(PrescriptionConfig.org_id == org_id,
                                             PrescriptionConfig.is_deleted.is_(False))
        )).scalars().all()
        cfg = {r.config_key: r.config_value for r in rows}
        await RedisHelper.set_json("m10:rx:config:%d" % org_id, cfg, ttl=600)
        return cfg

    async def _fetch_optometry(self, optometry_no: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """调 M10-01 P-01 取验光结果；验光单不可用报 1002020。"""
        try:
            data = await self.optical.__class__.fetch_optometry(self, optometry_no)
        except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001
            raise BizError("1002020", "验光服务不可用，请稍后重试或手工录入") from exc
        if not data or data.get("status") == "VOID":
            raise BizError("1002020", "验光单不存在或已作废：%s" % optometry_no)
        return data

    async def _audit_has_error(self, db: AsyncSession, rx: Prescription) -> bool:
        """提交审核前跑一次规则预检，判断是否存在 ERROR 命中。"""
        from app.services.m10.prescription_audit_service import PrescriptionRuleEngine
        engine = PrescriptionRuleEngine()
        result = await engine.evaluate(db, rx)
        return result.get("error_hit_count", 0) > 0

    async def _create_pending_audit(self, db: AsyncSession, rx: Prescription,
                                    payload) -> None:
        """创建 PENDING 审核记录（含规则命中快照）。"""
        from app.services.m10.prescription_audit_service import PrescriptionRuleEngine
        engine = PrescriptionRuleEngine()
        result = await engine.evaluate(db, rx)
        day = date.today().strftime("%Y%m%d")
        seq = await RedisHelper.incr("m10:code:RXA:%s" % day)
        audit_no = "RXA%s%05d" % (day, seq)
        rx.latest_audit_id = None
        rx.audit_level = payload.audit_level or result.get("suggested_audit_level", "SENIOR")
        db.add(PrescriptionLog(rx_id=rx.id, rx_no=rx.rx_no, action="SUBMIT_AUDIT",
                               audit_id=None, to_status="PENDING_AUDIT",
                               change_summary="创建待审记录 %s" % audit_no))
        return None

    async def _write_log(self, db: AsyncSession, rx: Prescription, action: str,
                        from_status: Optional[str] = None, to_status: Optional[str] = None,
                        operator=None, summary: Optional[str] = None,
                        reason: Optional[str] = None) -> None:
        """写处方操作日志（只增表）。"""
        db.add(PrescriptionLog(
            rx_id=rx.id, rx_no=rx.rx_no,
            rx_chain_root_id=rx.chain_root_rx_id,
            action=action, from_status=from_status, to_status=to_status,
            change_summary=summary, reason=reason,
            operator_id=getattr(operator, "id", None),
            operator_name=getattr(operator, "name", None),
            operator_ip=getattr(operator, "ip", None),
            result="SUCCESS",
        ))


# ============================================================================
# 审核 Service（规则引擎 + 审核动作 + P-07 回调）
# ============================================================================

class PrescriptionAuditService:
    """处方审核：规则引擎执行、审核动作、越权放行、P-07 回调。"""

    async def evaluate(self, db: AsyncSession, rx) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """逐条执行启用的审核规则，返回命中集合。"""
        rules = await self._load_rules(db, rx.org_id)
        ctx = self._build_context(rx)
        error_hits, warn_hits, info_hits = [], [], []
        for rule in rules:
            if not rule.applies_to(rx.lens_type, rx.age, rx.rx_source):
                continue
            hits = self._run_rule(rule, ctx)
            for hit in hits:
                {"ERROR": error_hits, "WARN": warn_hits, "INFO": info_hits}[
                    rule.severity].append(hit)
        severity_max = ("ERROR" if error_hits
                        else "WARN" if warn_hits
                        else "INFO" if info_hits else None)
        return {
            "error_hits": error_hits, "warn_hits": warn_hits, "info_hits": info_hits,
            "error_hit_count": len(error_hits), "warn_hit_count": len(warn_hits),
            "severity_max": severity_max,
            "can_submit": not error_hits,
            "can_pass": not error_hits,
            "suggested_audit_level": self._suggest_level(rx, error_hits, warn_hits),
        }

    async def audit(self, db: AsyncSession, user, rx_no: str, payload,
                    idempotency_key: Optional[str] = None) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """审核通过 / 带说明通过 / 驳回；儿童首配走双人复核。"""
        preset = await PrescriptionService()._get_or_raise(db, rx_no, with_items=True)
        if preset.status != "PENDING_AUDIT":
            raise BizError("1002006", "当前状态（%s）不允许审核" % preset.status)
        if preset.version != payload.version:
            raise BizError("40900", "处方已被他人修改，请刷新后重试")
        result = await self.evaluate(db, preset)

        if payload.audit_result == "REJECT":
            if not payload.reject_reason or len(payload.reject_reason.strip()) < 10:
                raise BizError("1002013", "驳回原因不能少于 10 个字")
            preset.status = "REJECTED"
            preset.reject_reason = payload.reject_reason
        else:
            if result["error_hit_count"] > 0:
                first = result["error_hits"][0]
                raise BizError("1002011",
                               "存在错误级规则命中，不可审核通过：%s %s"
                               % (first["rule_code"], first["rule_name"]))
            if result["warn_hit_count"] > 0 and not payload.audit_note:
                raise BizError("1002010", "命中警告规则，请填写审核说明")
            need_double = preset.audit_level == "DOUBLE"
            if need_double and preset.audited_by and preset.audited_by != user.id:
                # 第二位审核人完成 → 正式签发
                preset.status = "AUDITED"
            elif need_double:
                # 第一位审核人 → 保持待审，等待第二人复核
                preset.audited_by = user.id
                preset.audited_by_name = user.name
                await db.commit()
                return {"rx_no": preset.rx_no, "status": "PENDING_AUDIT",
                        "status_label": "待双人复核", "need_second_audit": True}
            else:
                preset.status = "AUDITED"

        preset.audited_by = preset.audited_by or user.id
        preset.audited_by_name = preset.audited_by_name or user.name
        preset.audited_at = datetime.now()
        preset.audited_result = payload.audit_result
        preset.version += 1
        if preset.status == "AUDITED":
            preset.issued_at = datetime.now()
            preset.sign_hash = PrescriptionService.build_sign_hash(preset.to_sign_payload())
        await db.commit()

        payload_evt = {"rx_no": preset.rx_no, "audit_result": payload.audit_result,
                       "valid_until": preset.valid_until.isoformat()}
        if preset.status == "AUDITED":
            await self._publish("m10.prescription.issued", payload_evt)
        await self._publish("m10.prescription.audited", payload_evt)
        return {"rx_no": preset.rx_no, "status": preset.status,
                "audit_result": payload.audit_result, "version": preset.version}

    async def on_audited_callback(self, db: AsyncSession, caller: str,
                                  payload: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """P-07：外部审核结果回调，按 (rx_no, audit_no) 幂等。"""
        rx_no = payload.get("rx_no")
        audit_no = payload.get("audit_no")
        rx = await PrescriptionService()._get_or_raise(db, rx_no, with_items=True)

        existing = (await db.execute(
            select(func.count()).select_from(PrescriptionLog).where(
                PrescriptionLog.rx_no == rx_no,
                PrescriptionLog.action.in_(["AUDIT_PASS", "AUDIT_REJECT"]),
                PrescriptionLog.change_summary.like("%" + str(audit_no) + "%"))
        )).scalar_one()
        if existing:
            return {"rx_no": rx_no, "accepted": False, "idempotent": True,
                    "status": rx.status,
                    "valid_until": rx.valid_until.isoformat(),
                    "supply_blocked": rx.supply_check_status in ("PARTIAL", "NONE_AVAILABLE")}

        audit_result = payload.get("audit_result")
        if audit_result in ("PASS", "PASS_WITH_NOTE"):
            rx.status = "AUDITED"
            rx.audited_result = audit_result
            rx.audited_at = datetime.now()
            rx.issued_at = rx.issued_at or datetime.now()
            rx.sign_hash = rx.sign_hash or PrescriptionService.build_sign_hash(
                rx.to_sign_payload())
            await self._publish("m10.prescription.issued",
                               {"rx_no": rx.rx_no, "audit_result": audit_result})
        elif audit_result == "REJECT":
            rx.status = "REJECTED"
            rx.reject_reason = payload.get("note") or "外部系统审核驳回"
        rx.version += 1
        await db.commit()
        return {"rx_no": rx_no, "accepted": True, "idempotent": False,
                "status": rx.status, "valid_until": rx.valid_until.isoformat(),
                "supply_blocked": rx.supply_check_status in ("PARTIAL", "NONE_AVAILABLE")}

    async def _load_rules(self, db: AsyncSession, org_id: int) -> List[Any]:
        """加载启用规则（Redis 缓存 30 分钟，按 priority 升序）。"""
        from app.models.m10.prescription import PrescriptionAuditRule
        rows = (await db.execute(
            select(PrescriptionAuditRule)
            .where(PrescriptionAuditRule.org_id == org_id,
                   PrescriptionAuditRule.status == "ENABLED",
                   PrescriptionAuditRule.is_deleted.is_(False))
            .order_by(PrescriptionAuditRule.priority)
        )).scalars().all()
        return list(rows)

    @staticmethod
    def _build_context(rx) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """构造规则引擎求值上下文（含双眼度数、偏差、球镜等效、屈光参差）。"""
        ctx: Dict[str, Any] = {"age": rx.age, "lens_type": rx.lens_type,
                               "rx_source": rx.rx_source, "eyes": {}}
        ses = {}
        for item in rx.items:
            if item.is_deleted:
                continue
            ctx["eyes"][item.eye_side] = {
                "sphere": float(item.sphere), "cylinder": float(item.cylinder),
                "axis": item.axis,
                "add_power": float(item.add_power) if item.add_power is not None else None,
                "va_cc": float(item.va_cc) if item.va_cc is not None else None,
                "pd": float(item.pd) if item.pd is not None else None,
                "ph": float(item.ph) if item.ph is not None else None,
                "prism_value": float(item.prism_value) if item.prism_value is not None else 0.0,
                "deviation_sph": float(item.deviation_sph or 0),
                "deviation_cyl": float(item.deviation_cyl or 0),
                "deviation_axis": item.deviation_axis or 0,
            }
            ses[item.eye_side] = float(item.sphere_equivalent or 0)
        ctx["anisometropia"] = (abs(ses.get("OD", 0) - ses.get("OS", 0))
                                if len(ses) == 2 else 0.0)
        return ctx

    @staticmethod
    def _run_rule(rule, ctx: Dict[str, Any]) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """执行单条规则；受限 DSL 由白名单函数集求值，禁止 eval 任意表达式。"""
        params = dict(rule.params or {})
        hits: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
        scope = ctx["eyes"] if rule.eye_scope != "BOTH" else {"BOTH": ctx["eyes"]}
        for eye, data in scope.items():
            env = dict(params)
            env.update(data)
            env["anisometropia"] = ctx["anisometropia"]
            env["age"] = ctx["age"]
            try:
                matched = bool(rule_evaluate(rule.expression, env))
            except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001  规则表达式异常不影响主流程
                continue
            if matched:
                hits.append({
                    "rule_code": rule.rule_code, "rule_name": rule.rule_name,
                    "severity": rule.severity, "eye_side": eye,
                    "error_code": rule.error_code,
                    "message": (rule.message_template or rule.rule_name),
                    "suggestion": rule.suggestion_text,
                })
        return hits

    @staticmethod
    def _suggest_level(rx, error_hits: List[Dict[str, Any]],
                       warn_hits: List[Dict[str, Any]]) -> str:
        """建议审核级别：儿童首配 → DOUBLE；渐进/棱镜 → SENIOR；否则 SELF。"""
        if rx.age_group == "CHILD":
            return "DOUBLE"
        if rx.lens_type in ("PROGRESSIVE", "BIFOCAL", "PRISM"):
            return "SENIOR"
        if error_hits or warn_hits:
            return "SENIOR"
        return "SELF"

    @staticmethod
    async def _publish(topic: str, payload: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:
        """发布跨模块事件（失败不阻断主流程，落 outbox 补投）。"""
        from app.core.events import EventPublisher
        await EventPublisher.publish(topic, payload, source="M10-02")


def rule_evaluate(expression: str, env: Dict[str, Any]) -> bool:
    """受限表达式求值：仅允许算术比较与白名单字段，避免任意代码执行。"""
    allowed_names = {
        "abs": abs, "min": min, "max": max, "round": round,
        "True": True, "False": False,
    }
```
